1b1('dul ba): @##//rgya gar skad du/bi na yuk+Sha dra ka bstu/bod skad du/'dul ba phran tshegs 
1b2('dul ba): kyi gzhi//bam po dang po//thams cad mkhyen pa la phyag 'tshal lo//phran tshegs kyi 
1b3('dul ba): gzhi'i sdom ni/so phag chad pa chos gos dang //shing tshad dang ni gnas brtan lus 
1b4('dul ba): rgyus pa 'gyod pa 'byung ba nyid//phran tshegs gzhir ni yang dag bshad//
2a1('dul ba): @##//bsdus pa'i sdom ni/so phag sen mo bcos pa dang //lhung bzed yan lag me long skyes//lam 
2a2('dul ba): phan chu tshags mon sran sde'u//rkang pa bkru dang gos mdud do//sdom ni/so phag dang ni sa gar dang //nya phyis dri yi lag 
2a3('dul ba): ris dang //ka ba dang ni skud pa gnyis//lag rgyan dang ni sor gdub rgya//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//yengs pa 
2a4('dul ba): can na spre'u rdzing gi 'gram//khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas na bzhugs so//drug sde nyid rnams snga dro sham thabs dang /
2a5('dul ba): chos gos bgos lhung bzed thogs te/yangs pa can du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /yangs pa can 
2b1('dul ba): gyi bar dang /khang pa brtsegs pa'i bar gyi bar skabs de na/yangs pa can gyi ling tsha byi rnams kyi bskyed mos tshal me tog dang 'bras bu 
2b2('dul ba): dang /chu phun sum tshogs pa/bya sna tshogs skad 'byin pa dga' ba'i tshal dang 'dra ba de dag na de dag gi brtsal ba dang /gar dang /
2b3('dul ba): glu dang /rol mo'i sgra'i cha spyad sna tshogs dag dang /khrus kyi yo byad sna tshogs dag kyang bzhag pa dang /drug sde rnams kyi smras pa/
2b4('dul ba): dga' bo nye dga' bo//yangs pa can gyi ling che byi 'di dag dang /bskyed mos tshal gyi dga' ba ni/bcom ldan 'das kyis/sum 
2b5('dul ba): cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams dper mdzad na/re zhig de dag gi bskyed mos tshal/ci 'dra ba blta'o zhes/de rnams blod nas de dag gi 
2b6('dul ba): bskyed mos tshal du zhugs so//de dag gis de na brtsal ba'i yo byad mda' bo che dang /drang ba dang /'phang rdo sna tshogs dang /gar dang /
3a1('dul ba): @##//glu dang /rol mo'i sgra'i cha spyad sna tshogs dang /pi wang dang /gling bu dang /rdza rnga dang /mkhar rnga dang /skad 
3a2('dul ba): snyan la sogs pa sna tshogs dang /khrus kyi ya byad so phag dang /bkru ba'i phye ma dang /mgo bkru ba skyu ru ra sna tshogs mthong ba dang /de rnams 
3a3('dul ba): kyis phan tshun smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo ya byad phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa du ma 'di dag yod na/ci re zhig brtsal ba bya 'am/'on te 
3a4('dul ba): gar bya 'am/glu blang ngam/rol mo'i sgra bya 'am/khrus bya/kha cig gis smras pa/bdag cag bkrus nas rang zhig lon gyis/ji khrus 
3a5('dul ba): bya'o zhes de dag blang nas so phag dag gis drud cing drud nas khrus bya bar brtsams pa dang /bzo'i gnas dang las kyi gnas de dag la mkhas pas/
3a6('dul ba): khrus la zhugs pa de rnams kyi sgra yid du 'ong ba 'di lta ste/sil snyan yan lag lnga pa skyes bu mkhas pas legs par dkrol ba'i 
3b1('dul ba): lta bu byung ngo//skye bo'i tshogs phal po che lam de nas 'gro bas/sgra de thos pa dang /de rnams rna ba byo ste yud tsam bsdad nas smras pa/shes ldan dag yangs pa can gyi yid 
3b2('dul ba): che byi rnams kyi bskyed mos tshal na/gar mkhan rnams gar byed pa ltar 'dod ngam/de ltar bya'o zhes ltad mo la yid 'dun pa de dag myur bar chas te/bskyed mos tshal du 
3b3('dul ba): dong nas/de dag nang du zhugs pa dang /drug sde rnams kyang phyir byung ngo//de dag gis de rnams la smras pa/'phags pa dag phyogs gang na/gar mkhan rnams gang 
3b4('dul ba): bgyid/de rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis bye brag mi phyed de/gar mkhan gar byed pa la sgra 'di lta bu 'byung ngam/'phags pa dag 'o na 'di su'i sgra lags/
3b5('dul ba): shes ldan dag kho bo cag lus drud nas khrus byas pa yin no//'phags pa dag ci dge sbyong khyed 'dod pa'i yon tan gyis gzir tam/bkren pa khyed kyi mgrin pa rdog pas 
3b6('dul ba): mnan la dge sbyong kho bo cag 'dod pa'i yon tan gyis bzir to//khyed kho bo cag gi rjes su sten pa yin nam ci/ma smra bar dong /khyed la gnod pa byas sa re zhes de 
3b7('dul ba): rnams kyis de dag bsdigs pa dang /sba gong nas dong ste/yangs pa can du zhugs nas/lam po che dang srang dang /bzhi mdo dang /sum mdo dag tu 'phya bar 
4a1('dul ba): @##//brtsams so//dge slong rnams kyis skabs de thos nas/bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa dang /nyes dmigs 
4a2('dul ba): gang chung zad byung ba ni/dge slong rnams phag dag gi lus drud de/khrus byed pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gis so phag gis lus drud de khrus mi bya'o//'drud 
4a3('dul ba): na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis so phag gi lus de khrus mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong dag rkang pa ser ga byung zhing dri 
4a4('dul ba): ma dang bcas bzhin bsod snyoms la 'jug pa de dag bram ze dang /khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas/smras pa/'phags pa dag khyed zhabs ser ga byung zhing dri ma dang 
4a5('dul ba): bcas na/ci ste dri ma sbyang bar mi bgyi/de rnams kyis smras pa/bzhin bzangs dag bcom ldan 'das kyis ma gnang ngo//ci 'phags pa khyed dri ma'i 'dam bcangs pas 
4a6('dul ba): dag par 'gyur ram/de rnams cang mi smra bar 'dug ste/bsod snyoms la brgyus nas/gtsug lag khang du dong ba dang /skabs de dge slong rnams kyis/bcom 
4a7('dul ba): ldan 'das la rgyas par gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de ltar na snga ma ni bcas pa'o//'di ni gnang ba yin te/de lta bas na dge slong 
4b1('dul ba): gis rkang pa so phag gis drud par gnang ngo //lus gzhan ni ma yin te/'drud na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das yangs pa can na spre'u rdzing gi 'gram khang pa brtsegs pa na 
4b2('dul ba): bzhugs so//dge slong rnams snga dro sham thabs dang /chos gos bgos/lhung bzed thogs te/yangs pa can du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /de rnams kyis bram ze zhags 
4b3('dul ba): dkar gyis so ris gsum byugs nas slongs mo la rgya zhing de'i bslangs pa yang mang du rnyed par mthong ngo //drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo thabs 'di ni bzang po zhig ste/
4b4('dul ba): bdag cag kyang de ltar bya'o zhes/de rnams phyi de nyin sa dkar gyis so ris gsum phyugs nas/bsod snyoms la zhugs pa ma phed pa rnams kyis mthong nas/'phya zhing /gus par smra'o//
4b5('dul ba): zhes smras pa dang /drug sde rnams kyis smras pa /bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis 
4b6('dul ba): smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin la/dge slong rnams ni phyag bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams 
4b7('dul ba): la gus par smra ba byed kyi phyag ma yin/'phags pa bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi dge slong du ni ma lags so//de lta lags pas bdag cag gis ma 'tshal 
5a1('dul ba): gyis brjod par gsol zhes byas pa dang /de dag cang mi smra bar 'dug go/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis thos nas/bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom 
5a2('dul ba): ldan 'das kyi dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ci byung ba ni dge slong rnams so ris gsum 'dri ba las gyur gyis de lta bas na dge slong gis so ris gsum bri bar mi bya'o//dge 
5a3('dul ba): slong so ris gsum 'dri na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis so ris gsung mi bya go zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong dag sman pas bsgo yang lus 
5a4('dul ba): sam mi sdud pa'i skabs de/dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de ltar na snga ma ni bcas pa'o//'di ni 
5a5('dul ba): gnang ba yin te/de lta bas na sman pas bsgo ba ni ma gtogs so//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi 
5a6('dul ba): kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro sha ma thabs dang chos gos bgos/lhung bzed thogs te/mnyan yod du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa na/de rnams kyis bram 
5a7('dul ba): ze 'gi'u wang gi thig le byas pa zhig skye bo phal po che'i tshogs kyis bskor cing ston mo'i bsngags pa brjod pa mthong ba dang /de rnams kyis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo thabs 'di 
5b1('dul ba): ni bzang po zhig ste/bdag cag kyang 'di ltar bya'o//legs kyi de ltar bya'o zhes de rnams phyi de nyin 'gi'u wang gi thig le dag gis bdag nyid brgyan pa dag byas nas/bsod snyoms la zhugs 
5b2('dul ba): pa dang |brem za dang khyim bdag dag gi mthong nas/'phya zhing gus par smra'o gus par smra'o zhes smras pa dang /drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang 
5b3('dul ba): dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba 
5b4('dul ba): yin la/dge slong rnams ni phyag bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams la gus par smra ba byed kyi/phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze legs par 
5b5('dul ba): 'tshal gyi/dge slong du ni ma lagaso//de'i slad du bdag cagi ma 'tshal gyis/bzod par gsol zhes byas pa dang /de dag cang mi smra bar 'dugo/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom 
5b6('dul ba): ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ci byung ba ni dge slong dag 'gi'u wang gi thig le dag gis bdag nyid brgyan pa las 
5b7('dul ba): gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gis ciga'u wang gi thig le dag gi sa brgyan par mi bya'o//brgyan na 'gal chabs can du 'gyuro//bcom ldan 'das kyis/dge slong gis 
6a1('dul ba): @#//'di'u wang gi thig le dag gis bdag nyid brgyan par mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong zhig 'phral bar rma byung ba de sman pa'i drung du song nas bzhin bzangs bdag 
6a2('dul ba): la sman ston cig/'phags pa rma'i thon kor 'gi'u wang gis bskus shig dang /khyod 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//des smras pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis ma gnang ngo//'phags pa khyod kyi ston pa 
6a3('dul ba): ni thugs rje can yin pas/gnang bar 'gyur ro//bstabs de dge slong rnams kyis/bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/de lta bas na dge 
6a4('dul ba): slong dag sman pas bsgo ba ni ma gtogs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis so phag gis lus drud par mi bya'o zhes bka' stsa pa dang /dge slong rnams nya phyis kyis 'drud par

6a7('dul ba): snams shig/de dag gis smras pa/gnas brtan ci khyed dris byugs sam//byugs so//gnas brtan khyed dris byugs rung ngam/rung yang rung mi rung yang rung ste/nged kyis ni 
6b1('dul ba): byugs so//de dag 'phya smod kha zer ba'i skabs de/dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ci byung ba ni 
6b2('dul ba): dris nyug pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong rnams kyis dris byug par mi bya'o//nyug na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams kyis dri byug par 
6b3('dul ba): mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa sgra dge slong nad pa zhig sman pa'i drung du song ste/bzhin bzangs bdag la sman ston cig/des smras pa/'phags pa dris byugs na khyod 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//
6b4('dul ba): bzhin bzangs ci kho bo 'dod pa spyod dam/'phags pa khyod kyi sman ni 'di yin te/gzhan gyis 'tsho bar mi nus so//skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom lngan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom 
6b5('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de lta bas na gnang gis/sman pa'i lung gis dri bcang bar bya'o//dge slong dag dris byugs nas dge 'dun gyi nang na 'dug cing /bram ze dang khyim 
6b6('dul ba): bdag 'ongs shing ljags pa rnams la/chos ston par byed/bram ze dang khyim bdag gi khyim rnams su yang 'gro bar byed pa'i skabs de/dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol 
6b7('dul ba): pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/ngas dge slong dri 'chang ba'i kun tu spyad pa'i chos bca' bar bya ste/dge slong dri 'chang bas dris byugs nas dge 'dun? gyi nang du 'dug par mi bya/
7a1('dul ba): @#//bram ze dang khyim bdag 'ongs shing lhags pa rnams la chos bstan par mi bya/bram ze dang /khyim bdag gi khyim rnams su 'gro bar mi bya'i/nam sos par gyur pa 
7a2('dul ba): de'i tshe khrus byas nas dri ma med par gyur pa dang /dge 'dun gyi nang du 'dug par bya/bram ze dang khyim bdaga'ongs shing lhags pa rnams pa chos bstan par bya/bram ze dang khyi bdag 
7a3('dul ba): gi khyim rnams su 'gro ba ra bya'o//dge slong dri 'chang bas kun tu spyad pa'o chos ji ltar bcas pa bzhin yang dag ba blangs te/mi 'dug na/'gal tshabs can du 'gyuro//bram 
7a4('dul ba): ze dang khyim bdag dad pa can dag dge slong rnams la dri 'bul ba na/dge slong rnams 'gyod nas mi len pa dang /de dag gis smras pa/'phags pa dag gang gi che sangs rgyas bcom 
7a5('dul ba): ldan 'das 'jig rten du byung bar ma gyur ba de'i tshe na/mu stegs can rnams yon gnas lags/da ni bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du 'byung bas/da ltar khyed yon gnas 
7a6('dul ba): lags na/gal te khyod kyis ma bzhes na ci bdag cag/'jig rten 'di nas 'jig rten pha rol tu dge ba'i lam rgyags ma mchis par mchi'am/bdag cag la thugs brtse 
7a7('dul ba): ba'i slad du bzhes shig/de dag gis smras pa/bcom ldan 'das la zhu'o//skabs de dge slong rnams kyis/bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
7b1('dul ba): stsal pa/blang bar bya'o//dge slong rnams kyis blangs nas de dag nyid kyi mdun du bor ba dang /de dag gis smras pa/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis/rin thang bzhin du nyo nyon khyed 
7b2('dul ba): kyis blangs te dor ram/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/blangs nas dor bar mi bya'i/'on kyang de bzhin 
7b3('dul ba): gshegs pa'i mchod rten la dri'i lag ris byug par bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong rnams kyis blangs pa nas/dbu skra dang sen mo'i mchod rten dag la dri'i lag ris nyug go/de 
7b4('dul ba): dag gis smras pa/'phags bdag ci bdag cag gis mchod rten ma mthong ngam/bdag cag gis ni khyed kho na la phul te/dbu skra dang sen mo'i ma chod rten dag la ni sngar phul lags so/
7b5('dul ba): skabs de dge slong rnams gyis/bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das gyis bka' stsal pa/blangs pa nas gnas khang la byug par bya'o//de rnams kyis gnas 
7b6('dul ba): khang gi sgo la byugs pa dang /de dag dri gtsang khang yin no snyam nas phyag 'tshal bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das
7b7('dul ba): kyis bka' stsal pa/nang du byug par bya'o//dge slong rnams kyis drang thad du byugs pa dang /nyes dmigs de nyid du gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/sngas 
8a1('dul ba): @#//dang nye ba'am rkang pa dang nye bar byug par bya zhing dri zhim po thams cad mig la phan pas dus dus su bsnams par bya ste/'di la 'gyod par mi bya'o//
8a2('dul ba): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das chu srin byis pa gsod kyi ri la 'jigs 'jigs lta'i tshal ri dgas kyi nags na bzhugs so//ji tsan na rgya lu gzhon nu rtogs byed kyis khang bzangs 
8a3('dul ba): padam ris byed du bcug nas/bcom ldan 'das nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa gdugs tshod la spyan drangs te/bcom ldan 'das khang bzangs 'od ma'i ris su dge slong gi dge 
8a4('dul ba): 'dun gyi gung la gdan bsham pa la bzhugs so//nye dga' bo zan za ba'i tshe lag pas ka ba la bsnun pa dang /khang bzangs padam ris g.yos par gyur to//'dri ma mkhan rnams 
8a5('dul ba): kyis smras pa/'phags pa rgyal bu rtogs byed kyis khang bzang padam ris tshon brgya rtsa gcig gis bkra bar byas pa/khyed kyis gzhig gam ci/nye dga' bos smras pa/
8a6('dul ba): bkren pa rtogs byed 'di la lhag par chags pas dus byas nas gar skya gtol mod//khyed kyang 'di la lhag par chags bas dus byas nas yi dgas wa ba can rnams kyi nang 
8a7('dul ba): du skye bar 'gyur ro//de dag kha zer ba'i skabs de/dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung 
8b1('dul ba): zad byung ba ni dge slong dag ka ba la lag pas bsnun pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gi lag pas ka ba la bsnun par mi bya'o//bsnun na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//
8b2('dul ba): bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis ka ba la lag pas bsnunapar mi bya zhes bka' stsal pa dang /drug sde rnams khu tshur dang /gru mo dang /phrag pa dang /mgo dang /rtsib logs 
8b3('dul ba): dang /rgyab dang /rked pa dang /brla dang /pus mo dang /phyin pa dang /rkang pa dang /dbyug pa dang /bong ba dang /gzhan gyis bsnun par byed pa nyes dmigs de nyid du gyur 
8b4('dul ba): nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gang gis kyang bsnun par mi bya'o//drug sde rnams rtsig pa dang /sa dang gzhan la bsnun par byed pa dang /bcom ldan 'das 
8b5('dul ba): kyis bka' stsal pa/gzhan la yang gang dag gis bsnun par mi bya'o//snun na 'gal tshabs can tu 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal 
8b6('dul ba): byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro mnyan yod du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /de rnams kyis bum ze zhig tshangs pa'i 
8b7('dul ba): skud pa thogs te/slongs mo la rgyu zhing /des bza' ba dang bca' ba yang mang du snyed pa mthong nas/drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/dga' ba nye dga' bo//thabs 'di ni bza' 
9a1('dul ba): @#//po zhig ste bdag cag kyang de lta bu bya'o zhes de dag phyi de nyin tshangs pa'i skud pa thogs te/bsod snyoms la zhugs pa ma dad pa rnams kyis mthong nas/'phya zhing gus par smra'o 
9a2('dul ba): gus par smra'o zhes smras pa dang /drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/bkrin pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag 
9a3('dul ba): gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin la/dge slong rnams ni phyag bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams la 
9a4('dul ba): gus par smra bar byed kyis phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi dge slong du ni ma lags so//de lta lags pas bdag cag gis ma 'tshal gyis 
9a5('dul ba): bzod par gsol zhes byas pa dang /de dag cang mi smra bar 'dug go/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis thos nas bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes 
9a6('dul ba): dmigs gang cung zad ci 'byung ba ni dge slong rnams tshangs pa'i skud pa thogs pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gis tshangs pa'i skud pa gdags par mi bya'o//'dogs na 'gal 
9a7('dul ba): tshabs can du 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro shan 
9b1('dul ba): thabs dang chos gos bgos lhung bzed thogs te/mnyan yod du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa na/de dag gis bram ze dri dang me tog gis brgyan pa tshon sna lnga pa'i skud pas lag pa bcings 
9b2('dul ba): pa zhig dus ston zos nas dbang po tshim ste/khyim nas byung ba mthong ba dang /de dag gis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo thabs 'di ni bzangs po zhig ste/bdag cag kyang 'di bzhin bya'o
